                                                     ModMed
                                                     Gastroenterology
                                                     gGastro EHI Patient Export Specifications
                                Version: 6.5.3.20251230
Introduction
The purpose of this specifications document is to describe the content of the package provided as part of the EHI
Patient Export output.

This document has been divided in five (5) distinctive parts:

        CSV Files Dictionary
         Describing the content of each CSV file included in the package, down to the field position and name, type
         of field, maximum length, format, translation table and comments.

        Data Schema
         Provides a tree like view of the relation between each CSV file needed to properly reconstruct the records
         along with the name of the field that links them.

        Translations
         Some specific fields are provided with a code that represents a pre-defined item from a value set. This
         section contains the translation tables to retrieve the human readable value for each code.

        Patient Documents
         Which describes how to relate the CSV files with their corresponding historical documents.

        Glossary
         List of terms and their definitions.

Patient Documents
The patient documents are included as part of the package provided along with the discrete data described in the
Data Schema chapter.

This is the list of document types and how to create a physical pointer to the target file.

Some of the pointers will use a reference to a specific Table and Field concatenation, while others will be based on
an specific folder structure and derived date for the record with an specific date format, or a combination of both.

Below the list of document types:

        Service Documents
         Encounters performed within the practice will generate a set of service documents, described in the tables
         with the same name: Service Document and Service Document Version..

         If the specific service document HAS A VERSION recorded in the Service Document Version table related
         by the ServiceDocumentId, then take the record marked by the field Current = 1 and assuming DATE =
         ServiceDocumentVersion.Date:

         Documents\FORMAT[DATE, YYYY]\Service\FORMAT[DATE, YYYYMMDD]\ServiceDocumentVersion.SnapshotFileName

         If the specific service document DOES NOT HAVE A VERSION recorded in the Service Document Version
         table, then take:

         ServiceDocument.SnapshotFileName
         but remove the GUID that precedes it.

         Example
         Documents\2023\Service\20230315\c21e5818-2aa8-4440-a714-8a46603472b8.mht


        Imaging Documents
         Corresponds to Inbound Faxes, Scanned Documents, Imported Documents, Lab Results (with an external
         document received) and others, as listed in the Imaging Document Source Type translation, easily
         determined by the document Source field in the record..

         In order to create the pointer concatenate the following values:

         ImagingDocument.Folder + ImagingDocumentPage.FileName

         Example
         Documents\2023\Scanned\20230315\34d95407-7412-4641-8786-b7e67d1d5681.jpg


        Patient Portal Documents
         The practice sends specific documents to the patient portal access point as per their designated workflows
         and compliance regulations..

         The full pointer can be found at:

         PatientPortalDocument.FilePath
    Example
    Documents\2023\Statement\20170903\63363178-7693-4f13-888e-ac4b4e05048e.pdf


   Letters
    These are document letters generated by the workflow for Recalls or any other type of business
    correspondence..

    The pointer can be concatenated assuming DATE = LetterQueue.Date:

    Documents\FORMAT[DATE, YYYY]\Letters\FORMAT[DATE, YYYYMMDD]\[LetterQueueFile.Id].mht

    Example
    Documents\2023\Letters\20170903\b0ec1df0-3f3b-4937-a272-1626be67e55c.mht
Glossary
AGA                American Gastroenterological Association.
CAHPS              Consumer Assessment of Healthcare Providers and Systems.
CPT                Current Procedural Terminology.
CSV                Comma Separated Values.
DOB                Date Of Birth.
GIQuIC             Gastro Intestinal Quality Improvement Consortium.
HCPCS              Healthcare Common Procedure Coding System.
ICD10              International Classification of Diseases 10th revision.
ICD9               International Classification of Diseases 9th revision.
Imaging Document   Any bitmap file used for several modules.
LOINC              Logical Observation Identifiers Names and Codes.
MRN                Medical Record Number typically assigned by the billing department to uniquely identify the
                   patient within the organization.
Order              Provider or Staff request for the patient to visit another physician, take a medication, follow up on
                   a given regimen, go to the lab or any other patient instructions.
Patient Kiosk      iOS based application for the patient to fill forms during registration.
Patient Portal     Patient specific application that allows an authorized patient to access the most vital medical
                   history, documents and overall communications as well as allow them to request an appointment.
PIF                Patient Interview Form.
ROS                Review Of Systems.
RXNORM             Medical Prescription Normalized.
Service            Encounter performed in the practice in between a provider, staff, patient and physical resources
                   like endoscopes, rooms and other equipment.
Service Template   XML based document template configured by the practice used to create a service document with
                   patient data.
SNOMED             Systematized Nomenclature of Medicine.
Task               Patient specific communication sent to a user or group of users in the practice in order to execute
                   an action or review a set of documents attached.
Translation        Mapping table to convert a NON-HUMAN readable code into its HUMAN-READABLE value.
